Cell["\<\
1. There is an external influence that is not under the control of the two \
players. This external random influence influences the payoffs . If we do not \
even know anything about the event distribution, we cannot even formulate \
this as an expected value problem. We could also formulate this as a 3-player \
problem. Except, the lion\[CloseCurlyQuote]s got nothing to win!

2. If we know the probability of the outcomes we can calculate the expected \
utility as in Problem 1:\
\>", "Text",
